<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic description report. Attribution: Jabir ibn Abdullah, as heard by Ata. The complete Hindi field reports the Prophet’s arrival in Makkah on the morning of 4 Dhul-Hijjah and the setting in which Ata heard it. The source spelling of Ata is retained.</p>

<p dir="auto">अत कहते हैं के जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने मुझसे मेरे साथ कई लोगों की मौजूदगी में हदीस बयान की के नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मक्काः मुकर्रमः ४ ज़िल-हज्जाह की सुबह तशरीफ़ लाये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1074]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

Keep that date and limited event without reconstructing a longer Hajj account.</p>

## Narration 1075

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A1075)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ أَبِي الشَّوَارِبِ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَاحِدِ بْنُ زِيَادٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَاصِمٌ الأَحْوَلُ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ أَقَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ تِسْعَةَ عَشَرَ يَوْمًا يُصَلِّي رَكْعَتَيْنِ رَكْعَتَيْنِ، فَنَحْنُ إِذَا أَقَمْنَا تِسْعَةَ عَشَرَ يَوْمًا، نُصَلِّي رَكْعَتَيْنِ رَكْعَتَيْنِ، فَإِذَا أَقَمْنَا أَكْثَرَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ، صَلَّيْنَا أَرْبَعًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1075]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1075" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Ibn ‘Abbas said:“The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) stayed for nineteen days in which he shortened his prayer to two Rak’ah. So, whenever we stayed for nineteen days we would shorten our prayer to two Rak’ah, but if we stayed more than that we would pray four Rak’ah.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1075]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1075" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) फ़तह मक्का के मौक़े पर मक्का में 19 दिन रहे, और दो-दो रकअत पढ़ते रहे, लिहाज़ा हम भी जब 19 दिन क़याम करते हैं तो दो-दो रकअत पढ़ते हैं, और जब इस से ज़्यादा क़याम करते हैं तो चार रकअत पढ़ते हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1075]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1075" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم فتح مکہ کے موقع پر مکہ میں انیس ( ۱۹ ) دن رہے، اور دو دو رکعت پڑھتے رہے، لہٰذا ہم بھی جب انیس ( ۱۹ ) دن قیام کرتے ہیں تو دو دو رکعت پڑھتے ہیں، اور جب اس سے زیادہ قیام کرتے ہیں تو چار رکعت پڑھتے ہیں ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1075]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1075" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Fatah Makkah ke mauqe par Makkah mein 19 din rahe, aur do-do rakaat padhte rahe, lihaza hum bhi jab 19 din qayam karte hain toh do-do rakaat padhte hain, aur jab is se zyada qayam karte hain toh chaar rakaat padhte hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1075]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1075" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
